METHOD System.String OneClickCraft.ModSettings/<>c::<Init>b__16_7()
IL_0000: ldstr "默认关：成品/备料一键到底（自动蒸发）。
打开后搅拌机面板多一颗「手动过夜」：一键做到「过滤后、蒸发前」就停手 —— 水和溶剂留在瓶里，把瓶子搬进蒸发器（带电池、馏出槽放空瓶）挂一晚，蒸发交给原生机制慢慢跑，酒精按品质加成回收。想自己体验/利用过夜蒸发的玩家开这个。"
IL_0005: ldstr "Off by default: Produce/Prep run all the way (auto evaporation).
When on, the blender panel gains an 'Overnight' button: it stops right after filtering, BEFORE evaporation — water and solvent stay in the bottle. Move it into the evaporator (with a battery and an empty bottle in the distillate slot) and let the native evaporation run overnight; alcohol is recycled by quality bonus.
Note: the button is injected once per blender — restart the game after toggling."
IL_000a: call System.String OneClickCraft.L::T(System.String,System.String)
IL_000f: ret

METHOD System.String OneClickCraft.ModSettings/<>c::<Init>b__16_9()
IL_0000: ldstr "默认关：一键运行只在失败时留一行原因，零输出零开销。
打开后记录完整流水（搅拌/溶剂/控温/中和/过滤/蒸发/提纯每一步的量与杂质）到游戏日志（Player.log）和游戏内化学全书的日志页签。异常始终记录，不受此开关影响。
遇到问题：把这项打开 → 复现一次 → 把 Player.log 发给作者即可。"
IL_0005: ldstr "Off by default: a failed run leaves a single reason line; zero output, zero overhead.
When on, the full pipeline log (blend / solvent / heat / neutralize / filter / evaporate / purify — volumes and impurities at every step) is written to the game log (Player.log) and the in-game codex log tab. Exceptions are always logged regardless.
For troubleshooting: turn this on, reproduce the issue once, then send Player.log to the author."
